SOV_russian_victory = {
    SOV = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_global_flag = DSW_SOV_european_war_started
                NOT = { has_global_flag = DSW_SOV_european_war_resolved }
                DSW_SOV_european_victory_conditions = yes
            }
            DSW_SOV_resolve_russian_victory = yes
        }
    }
}

SOV_increase_refining = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_national_resource_excavation1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_national_resource_excavation1
			add_idea = SOV_national_resource_excavation2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_national_resource_excavation2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_national_resource_excavation2
			add_idea = SOV_national_resource_excavation3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_national_resource_excavation3
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_national_resource_excavation3
			add_idea = SOV_national_resource_excavation4
		}
	}
}

SOV_increase_supremacy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_1
					has_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_2
					has_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_3
					has_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_4
					has_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_5
					has_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_6
					has_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_7
				}
			}
		}
		add_ideas = SOV_russian_supremacy_2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_1
			add_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_2
			add_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_3
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_3
			add_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_4
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_4
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_4
			add_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_5
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_5
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_5
			add_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_6
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_6
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_6
			add_idea = SOV_russian_supremacy_7
		}
	}
}
SOV_update_party_legitimacy = {
	set_variable = {
		var = SOV_party_legitimacy_stability
		value = SOV.stability
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		var = SOV_party_legitimacy_stability
		value = 35
	}
	set_variable = {
		var = SOV_party_legitimacy
		value = SOV_party_legitimacy_stability
	}
	set_variable = {
		var = SOV_party_legitimacy_popularity
		value = SOV.party_pop_array^4
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = SOV_party_legitimacy_popularity
		value = SOV.party_pop_array^18
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		var = SOV_party_legitimacy_popularity
		value = 25
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = SOV_party_legitimacy
		value = SOV_party_legitimacy_popularity
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = SOV_party_legitimacy
		value = SOV_party_legitimacy_other
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_party_legitimacy
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_party_legitimacy < 30
				}
			}
			country_event = {
				id = dsw_tfr_russia.1537
			}
		}
	}
}

add_total_international_campaign_slot = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = add_international_campaign_slot_tooltip
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = international_campaign_slot_total_var
				value = 0
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = international_campaign_slot_total_var
			value = international_campaign_slot_total_var_temp
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = international_campaign_slot_var
			value = international_campaign_slot_total_var_temp
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = unlocks_international_campaigns_tooltip
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = {
			var = international_campaign_slot_total_var
			value = international_campaign_slot_total_var_temp
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = international_campaign_slot_var
			value = international_campaign_slot_total_var_temp
		}
	}
}
SOV_increase_great_russian_chauvinism = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_1
					has_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_2
					has_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_3
					has_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_4
					has_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_5
					has_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_6
				}
			}
		}
		add_ideas = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_1
			add_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_2
			add_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_3
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_3
			add_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_4
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_4
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_4
			add_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_5
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_5
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_5
			add_idea = SOV_great_russian_chauvinism_6
		}
	}
}

SOV_add_cprf_anger = {
	add_to_variable = {
		var = SOV_cprf_anger
		value = SOV_cprf_anger_temp
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_cprf_anger
		min = -1
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = SOV_cprf_anger
					value = 4
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			country_event = {
				id = dsw_tfr_russia.386
				days = 15
				random_days = 10
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				SOV_cprf_anger_temp = 1
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_add_cprf_anger_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = {
				party_index = 4
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				party_popularity_increase = 0.03
			}
			change_relative_party_popularity = yes
			recalculate_party = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				SOV_cprf_anger_temp = 2
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_add_big_cprf_anger_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = {
				party_index = 4
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				party_popularity_increase = 0.06
			}
			change_relative_party_popularity = yes
			recalculate_party = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_decrease_cprf_anger_tt
	}
}

SOV_decrease_reforming_the_system = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_1
					has_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_2
					has_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_3
					has_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_4
					has_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_5
					has_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_6
					has_idea = SOV_brand_new_democracy_1
					has_idea = SOV_brand_new_democracy_2
				}
			}
		}
		add_ideas = SOV_refoming_the_system_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_1
			add_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_2
			add_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_3
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_3
			add_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_4
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_4
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_4
			add_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_5
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_5
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_5
			add_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_6
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_6
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_refoming_the_system_6
			add_idea = SOV_brand_new_democracy_1
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_brand_new_democracy_1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_brand_new_democracy_1
			add_idea = SOV_brand_new_democracy_2
		}
	}

					has_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_1
					has_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_2
					has_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_3
					has_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_4
					has_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_5
					has_idea = SOV_all_russian_peoples_front_1
					has_idea = SOV_all_russian_peoples_front_2
					has_idea = SOV_all_russian_peoples_front_3
				}
			}
		}
		add_ideas = SOV_centralizing_the_system_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_1
			add_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_2
			add_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_3
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_3
			add_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_4
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_4
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_4
			add_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_5
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_centralizing_the_system_5
		}
		remove_ideas = SOV_centralizing_the_system_5
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_all_russian_peoples_front_1
			add_idea = SOV_all_russian_peoples_front_2
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_all_russian_peoples_front_2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_all_russian_peoples_front_2
			add_idea = SOV_all_russian_peoples_front_3
		}
	}
}
SOV_join_ADS = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = SOV_ADS_member_flag
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = SOV_ADS_member_flag
		add_ideas = {
			SOV_ADS_member
		}
		remove_ideas = {
			ASEAN_member
			arab_league_member
		}
		SOV = {
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_ADS_number_of_members = 1
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_ADS_war_stability_factor_dynamic_var = 0.01
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_ADS_send_volunteers_tension_dynamic_var = -0.01
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_ADS_opinion_gain_monthly_same_ideology_factor_dynamic_var = 0.01
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_ADS_lend_lease_tension_dynamic_var = -0.01
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_ADS_production_factory_start_efficiency_factor_dynamic_var = 0.005
			}
		}
		add_to_array = {
			SOV.SOV_ads_members = THIS
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = SOV
			modifier = friendship_of_peoples
		}
		SOV = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = ROOT
				modifier = friendship_of_peoples
			}
		}
	}
}

SOV_join_brics = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = SOV_brics_member_flag
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = SOV_brics_member_flag
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = SOV_solidarity_and_respect
			}
			add_ideas = {
				SOV_brics_member_2
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_ideas = {
				SOV_brics_member
			}
		}
		remove_ideas = {
			ASEAN_member
			arab_league_member
		}
		SOV = {
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_brics_number_of_members = 1
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_brics_political_power_factor_dynamic_var = 0.005
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_brics_income_growth_factor_dynamic_var = 0.005
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_brics_trade_cost_for_target_factor_dynamic_var = -0.005
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_brics_trade_opinion_factor_dynamic_var = 0.005
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				CHI = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			CHI = {
				add_to_variable = {
					SOV_brics_number_of_members = 1
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					SOV_brics_political_power_factor_dynamic_var = 0.005
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					SOV_brics_income_growth_factor_dynamic_var = 0.005
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					SOV_brics_trade_cost_for_target_factor_dynamic_var = -0.005
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					SOV_brics_trade_opinion_factor_dynamic_var = 0.005
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_array = {
			SOV.SOV_brics_members = THIS
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = SOV
			modifier = friendship_of_peoples
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = CHI
			modifier = friendship_of_peoples
		}
		SOV = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = PREV
				modifier = friendship_of_peoples
			}
		}
		CHI = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = PREV
				modifier = friendship_of_peoples
			}
		}
	}
}
SOV_leave_ADS = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = SOV_ADS_member_flag
		}
		clr_country_flag = SOV_ADS_member_flag
		remove_ideas = {
			SOV_ADS_member
		}
		SOV = {
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_ADS_number_of_members = -1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					SOV = {
						has_completed_focus = SOV_reinstate_comecon
					}
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					SOV_ADS_war_stability_factor_dynamic_var = -0.005
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					SOV_ADS_send_volunteers_tension_dynamic_var = 0.005
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					SOV_ADS_opinion_gain_monthly_same_ideology_factor_dynamic_var = -0.005
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					SOV_ADS_lend_lease_tension_dynamic_var = 0.005
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = {
					SOV_ADS_war_stability_factor_dynamic_var = -0.01
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					SOV_ADS_send_volunteers_tension_dynamic_var = 0.01
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					SOV_ADS_opinion_gain_monthly_same_ideology_factor_dynamic_var = -0.01
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					SOV_ADS_lend_lease_tension_dynamic_var = 0.01
				}
			}
		}
		remove_from_array = {
			SOV.SOV_ads_members = THIS
		}
	}
}

		}
		add_to_variable = {

		}
		add_to_variable = {

		}

		}
	}

SOV_increase_factories = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_completed_focus = SOV_a_new_republic
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = SOV_more_factories
			}
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = {
					building = industrial_complex
					size > 0
					include_locked = yes
				}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = {
					building = industrial_complex
					size > 0
					include_locked = yes
				}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_completed_focus = SOV_a_new_republic
			has_country_flag = SOV_more_factories
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = {
					building = industrial_complex
					size > 0
					include_locked = yes
				}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 2
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = {
					building = industrial_complex
					size > 0
					include_locked = yes
				}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

					NOT = {

							has_country_flag = SOV_nationalization_flag_2

				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state2_comm_dev_soc
					add_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state3_comm_dev_soc
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state3
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state3_comm_dev_soc
					add_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state4_comm_dev_soc
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state4
				}
				remove_ideas = SOV_kleptocratic_state4_comm_dev_soc
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				SOV = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_nationalization_flag_2
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state_comm_chi_soc
					add_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state2_comm_chi_soc
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state2
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state2_comm_chi_soc
					add_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state3_comm_chi_soc
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state3
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state3_comm_chi_soc
					add_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state4_comm_chi_soc
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state4
				}
				remove_ideas = SOV_kleptocratic_state4_comm_chi_soc
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state
				add_idea = SOV_kleptocratic_state2
			}
		}
		else_if = {

SOV_supreme_soviet_calculate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_other_parties_count
			value = 0
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = SOV_other_parties
			value = v
			break = break
			add_to_variable = {
				var = SOV_other_parties_count
				value = 1
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_other_parties_var
			value = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = SOV_other_parties
					value = BOL.id
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = SOV_other_parties_var
				value = SOV_adl_progress_opinion_var
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = SOV_other_parties
					value = BRA.id
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = SOV_other_parties_var
				value = SOV_labor_fatherland_opinion_var
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = SOV_other_parties
					value = ARG.id
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = SOV_other_parties_var
				value = SOV_peoples_front_opinion_var
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = SOV_other_parties
					value = GER.id
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = SOV_other_parties_var
				value = SOV_ldpr_opinion_var
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = SOV_other_parties
					value = FRA.id
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = SOV_other_parties_var
				value = SOV_patriots_party_opinion_var
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = SOV_other_parties
							value = BOL.id
						}
						is_in_array = {
							array = SOV_other_parties
							value = BRA.id
						}
						is_in_array = {
							array = SOV_other_parties
							value = ARG.id
						}
						is_in_array = {
							array = SOV_other_parties
							value = GER.id
						}
						is_in_array = {
							array = SOV_other_parties
							value = FRA.id
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = SOV_other_parties_var
				value = 5
			}
		}
		else = {
			divide_variable = {
				var = SOV_other_parties_var
				value = SOV_other_parties_count
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_supreme_soviet_political_power_gain_var
			value = SOV_other_parties_var
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = SOV_supreme_soviet_political_power_gain_var
			value = 0.03
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_supreme_soviet_party_popularity_stability_factor_var
			value = SOV_other_parties_var
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = SOV_supreme_soviet_party_popularity_stability_factor_var
			value = 0.03
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_supreme_soviet_war_support_factor_var
			value = SOV_other_parties_var
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = SOV_supreme_soviet_war_support_factor_var
			value = 0.03
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_supreme_soviet_society_development_monthly_var
			value = SOV_other_parties_var
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = SOV_supreme_soviet_society_development_monthly_var
			value = 0.004
		}
	}
}

SOV_increase_azarenok_influence = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = SOV_killed_azarenok_flag
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_increase_azarenok_influence_tt
		add_to_variable = {
			var = SOV_azarenok_gambit_var
			value = 1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_azarenok_gambit_var = 5
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				country_event = {
					id = dsw_tfr_russia.2108
					days = 135
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
SOV_increase_government_restructuring = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_government_restructuring_1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_government_restructuring_1
			add_idea = SOV_government_restructuring_2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_government_restructuring_2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_government_restructuring_2
			add_idea = SOV_government_restructuring_3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_government_restructuring_3
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_government_restructuring_3
			add_idea = SOV_government_restructuring_4
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_government_restructuring_4
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_government_restructuring_4
			add_idea = SOV_government_restructuring_5
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_government_restructuring_5
		}
		remove_ideas = SOV_government_restructuring_5
		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
	}
}

SOV_election_var_balancer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				target = 1
			}
		}
		divide_temp_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var_temp = 5
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var_temp = -1
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var = SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var_temp
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var = SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var_temp
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var = SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var_temp
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				target = 2
			}
		}
		divide_temp_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var_temp = 5
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var_temp = -1
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var = SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var_temp
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var = SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var_temp
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var = SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var_temp
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				target = 3
			}
		}
		divide_temp_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var_temp = 5
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var_temp = -1
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var = SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var_temp
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var = SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var_temp
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var = SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var_temp
		}
	}
	else = {
		divide_temp_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var_temp = 5
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var_temp = -1
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var = SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var_temp
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var = SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var_temp
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var = SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var_temp
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = {
		total_popularity = 0
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var
		min = 0
		max = 1
	}
	add_to_temp_variable = {
		total_popularity = SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var
		min = 0
		max = 1
	}
	add_to_temp_variable = {
		total_popularity = SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var
		min = 0
		max = 1
	}
	add_to_temp_variable = {
		total_popularity = SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var
		min = 0
		max = 1
	}
	add_to_temp_variable = {
		total_popularity = SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				total_popularity > 1
			}
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = {
			total_popularity = 1
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				114 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						check_variable = {
							SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var < total_popularity
						}
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var = total_popularity
					}
				}
				114 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						check_variable = {
							SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var < total_popularity
						}
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var = total_popularity
					}
				}
				114 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						check_variable = {
							SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var < total_popularity
						}
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var = total_popularity
					}
				}
				114 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						check_variable = {
							SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var < total_popularity
						}
					}
					subtract_from_variable = {
						SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var = total_popularity
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				total_popularity < 1
			}
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = {
			total_popularity = 1
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = {
			total_popularity = -1
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				114 = {
					add_to_variable = {
						SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var = total_popularity
					}
				}
				114 = {
					add_to_variable = {
						SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var = total_popularity
					}
				}
				114 = {
					add_to_variable = {
						SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var = total_popularity
					}
				}
				114 = {
					add_to_variable = {
						SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var = total_popularity
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = {
		SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var_temp = 0

SOV_onf_ideology_picker = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_leader = {
				character = DSW_TFR_SOV_dmitry_medvedev
				ruling_only = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_leader_ideology = putinism
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_integralist_score_var > 4
				}
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						SOV_left_statists_score_var > 2
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						SOV_national_patriots_score_var > 2
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						SOV_reformers_score_var > 2
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_leader_ideology = Conservative
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_integralist_score_var > 4
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_left_statists_score_var > 3
				}
			}
			set_country_leader_ideology = Conservative
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_integralist_score_var > 4
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_national_patriots_score_var > 3
				}
			}
			set_country_leader_ideology = Conservative
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_integralist_score_var > 4
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_reformers_score_var > 3
				}
			}
			set_country_leader_ideology = Conservative
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_left_statists_score_var > 4
				}
			}
			set_country_leader_ideology = Conservative
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_national_patriots_score_var > 4
				}
			}
			set_country_leader_ideology = Conservative
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_reformers_score_var > 4
				}
			}
			set_country_leader_ideology = Conservative
		}
	}
}

		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_industrial_capacity_factory_var
			value = 0.075
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_army_defence_factor_var
			value = 0.07
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				SOV_soviet_revivalists_score_var = 10
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_political_power_factor_var
			value = -0.08
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_industrial_capacity_factory_var
			value = 0.08
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_army_defence_factor_var
			value = 0.075
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				SOV_soviet_revivalists_score_var = 11
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_political_power_factor_var
			value = -0.085
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_industrial_capacity_factory_var
			value = 0.085
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_army_defence_factor_var
			value = 0.08
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				SOV_soviet_revivalists_score_var = 12
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_political_power_factor_var
			value = -0.09
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_industrial_capacity_factory_var
			value = 0.09
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_army_defence_factor_var
			value = 0.085
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				SOV_soviet_revivalists_score_var = 13
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_political_power_factor_var
			value = -0.1
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_industrial_capacity_factory_var
			value = 0.095
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_strength_dynamic_army_defence_factor_var
			value = 0.09
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_reformers_score_var = 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_populists_score_var = 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_integralist_score_var = 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_left_statists_score_var = 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_soviet_revivalists_score_var = 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_national_patriots_score_var = 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_kadyrovites_score_var = 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_z_patriots_score_var = 8
				}
			}
		}
		add_ideas = SOV_overpowered_faction_idea
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_reformers_score_var < 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_populists_score_var < 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_integralist_score_var < 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_left_statists_score_var < 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_soviet_revivalists_score_var < 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_national_patriots_score_var < 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_kadyrovites_score_var < 8
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_z_patriots_score_var < 8
				}
			}
			has_idea = SOV_overpowered_faction_idea
		}
		remove_ideas = SOV_overpowered_faction_idea
	}
}

		var = SOV_integralist_score_var
		min = 0
		max = 13
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_left_statists_score_var
		min = 0
		max = 13
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_national_patriots_score_var
		min = 0
		max = 13
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_reformers_score_var
		min = 0
		max = 13
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_populists_score_var
		min = 0
		max = 13
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_kadyrovites_score_var
		min = 0
		max = 13
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_soviet_revivalists_score_var
		min = 0
		max = 13
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_z_patriots_score_var
		min = 0
		max = 13
	}
}
SOV_add_oligarch_approval_rating = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_add_oligarch_approval_rating_tooltip
	add_to_variable = {
		var = SOV_oligarch_approval_rating_var
		value = SOV_oligarch_approval_rating_var_temp
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_oligarch_approval_rating_var
		min = 0
		max = 1
	}
	set_variable = {
		var = SOV_oligarch_support_dynamic_var
		value = SOV_oligarch_approval_rating_var
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		var = SOV_oligarch_support_dynamic_var
		value = 0.25
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = SOV_oligarch_support_dynamic_var
		min = 0
		max = 1
	}
}

SOV_who_won_the_election = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var > SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var > SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var > SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var > 0.5
				}
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = SOV_cprf_won
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var > SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var > SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var > SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var > 0.5
				}
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = SOV_ldpr_won
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			AND = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var > SOV_election_approval_rating_cprf_var
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var > SOV_election_approval_rating_ldpr_var
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var > SOV_election_approval_rating_ur_var
				}
				check_variable = {
					SOV_election_approval_rating_srzp_var > 0.5
				}
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = SOV_srzp_won
	}
	else = {
		set_global_flag = SOV_medvedev_won
	}
}

SOV_change_left_wing_power = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = SOV_left_wing_opposition_power_modifier
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_power_change > 0
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = {
						SOV_power_change < 0
					}
					check_variable = {
						SOV_left_opposition_power > 0
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			SOV_power_lower_limit = 1
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = {
			SOV_power_lower_limit = SOV_left_opposition_power
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			SOV_power_upper_limit = 10
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = {
			SOV_power_upper_limit = SOV_left_opposition_power
		}
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = SOV_power_change
			min = SOV_power_lower_limit
			max = SOV_power_upper_limit
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_variable = SOV_left_opposition_power
					}
					NOT = {
						check_variable = {
							SOV_power_change = 0
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						SOV_power_change > 0
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_increase_left_power
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_decrease_left_power
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				SOV_left_power_pp_temp = 0.02
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				SOV_left_power_pp_temp = SOV_power_change
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				SOV_left_power_stability_temp = -0.02
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				SOV_left_power_stability_temp = SOV_power_change
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				SOV_left_power_factory_output_temp = -0.02
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				SOV_left_power_factory_output_temp = SOV_power_change
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_left_opposition_power = SOV_power_change
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_left_power_pp = SOV_left_power_pp_temp
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_left_power_stability = SOV_left_power_stability_temp
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_left_power_factory_output = SOV_left_power_factory_output_temp
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = SOV_left_opposition_power
				value = 7
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_democratic_opposition_power > 5
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = SOV_killed_them_all_left_flag
			}
			NOT = {
				has_active_mission = SOV_democratic_uprising_mission
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_left_opposition_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			activate_mission = SOV_socialist_uprising_mission
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = SOV_left_opposition_power
				value = 7
				compare = equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				SOV_democratic_opposition_power > 5
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = SOV_killed_them_all_left_flag
			}
			NOT = {
				has_active_mission = SOV_democratic_uprising_mission
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_left_opposition_stalinist_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			activate_mission = SOV_socialist_uprising_mission
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_left_opposition_power > 7
				}
				has_active_mission = SOV_socialist_uprising_mission
			}
			add_days_mission_timeout = {
				mission = SOV_socialist_uprising_mission
				days = -10
			}
		}
	}
}
SOV_change_democratic_power = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = SOV_dem_opposition_power_modifier
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_power_change > 0
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = {
						SOV_power_change < 0
					}
					check_variable = {
						SOV_democratic_opposition_power > 0
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			SOV_power_lower_limit = 1
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = {
			SOV_power_lower_limit = SOV_democratic_opposition_power
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			SOV_power_upper_limit = 10
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = {
			SOV_power_upper_limit = SOV_democratic_opposition_power
		}
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = SOV_power_change
			min = SOV_power_lower_limit
			max = SOV_power_upper_limit
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_variable = SOV_democratic_opposition_power
					}
					NOT = {
						check_variable = {
							SOV_power_change = 0
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						SOV_power_change > 0
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_increase_dem_opposition_power
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_decrease_dem_opposition_power
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				SOV_dem_opposition_power_pp_temp = 0.02
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				SOV_dem_opposition_power_pp_temp = SOV_power_change
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				SOV_dem_opposition_power_stability_temp = -0.02
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				SOV_dem_opposition_power_stability_temp = SOV_power_change
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				SOV_dem_opposition_power_organisation_temp = -0.02
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				SOV_dem_opposition_power_organisation_temp = SOV_power_change
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_democratic_opposition_power = SOV_power_change
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_dem_opposition_power_pp = SOV_dem_opposition_power_pp_temp
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_dem_opposition_power_stability = SOV_dem_opposition_power_stability_temp
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				SOV_dem_opposition_power_organisation = SOV_dem_opposition_power_organisation_temp
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = SOV_democratic_opposition_power
				value = 7
				compare = equals
			}
			NOT = {
				has_active_mission = SOV_socialist_uprising_mission
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = SOV_killed_disloyal_generals_flag
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_democratic_opposition_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			activate_mission = SOV_democratic_uprising_mission
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					SOV_democratic_opposition_power > 7
				}
				has_active_mission = SOV_democratic_uprising_mission
			}
			add_days_mission_timeout = {
				mission = SOV_democratic_uprising_mission
				days = -10
			}
		}
	}
}
SOV_increase_corona = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea4
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea4
			add_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea3
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea3
			add_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea2
			add_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea1
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea1
			add_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea
		}
		add_stability = -0.05
	}
	else = {
		add_ideas = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea4
	}
}
SOV_decrease_corona = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea4
		}
		remove_ideas = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea4
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea3
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea3
			add_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea4
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea2
			add_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea1
			add_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea
			add_idea = SOV_coronavirus_pandemic_idea1
		}
		set_country_flag = SOV_corona_decision
	}
}
